Appendix A
Appendix

A.1
Proof of Proposition
2.1

Proof of Proposition
2.1
.

Assumption
1
is sufficient to guarantee the existence of the unique stationary distribution; see
[
15
]
(under their Assumption (A’)). The uniform PoC result follows from
[
45
, Theorem 1.2]
. This addresses the proposition’s last two statements.

The system’s wellposedness (as an SDE in
\mathbb{R}^{N}
) follows
[
45
]
. For completeness, we present a short proof for the moment stability of the IPS, highlighting that the constant
K
appearing in the RHS of the inequality is independent of
t
and
N
.

Let
p\geq 2
. Performing similar calculations as in
[
23
, Appendix A]
and applying Gronwall’s inequality, we deduce that there exists some positive constant
K
independent of
N,t\geq 0
, such that for any
i\neq j

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\,|X^{i,N}_{t}-X^{j,N}_{t}|^{p}]\leq K<\infty.

(A.1)

Employing Itô’s formula, we deduce for any
t\geq 0
,
p\geq 2
and
\kappa\in[0,\lambda)

\displaystyle e^{p\kappa t}|X^{i,N}_{t}|^{p}-|\xi^{i}|^{p}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}p\bigg(\kappa|X_{s}^{i,N}|^{2}+\big(X_{s}^{i,N}\big)\cdot\Big(-\nabla U(X_{s}^{i,N})-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(X_{s}^{i,N}-X_{s}^{j,N})\Big)\bigg)e^{p\kappa s}|X^{i,N}_{s}|^{p-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\frac{p(p-1)}{2}\int_{0}^{t}e^{p\kappa s}|X_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\mathrm{d}s+\sigma p\int_{0}^{t}e^{p\kappa s}|X_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}X_{s}^{i,N}\mathrm{d}W_{s}^{i}.

Taking expectations on both sides, using the inequality: for
q\in\{1,2\}

\displaystyle a^{p-q}b^{q}\leq\frac{p-q}{p}\varepsilon a^{p}+\frac{q}{p\varepsilon^{(p-q)/q}}b^{p},\qquad\textrm{for any $\varepsilon>0$ and $a,b>0$},

assumption
1
and (
A.1
) yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[e^{p\kappa t}|X^{i,N}_{t}|^{p}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[|\xi^{i}|^{p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq p\int_{0}^{t}(\kappa-\lambda)\mathbb{E}\left[e^{p\kappa s}|X^{i,N}_{s}|^{p}\right]\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{t}e^{p\kappa s}\Big(\frac{K}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Big[|X^{i,N}_{s}|^{p-1}~|X^{i,N}_{s}-X^{j,N}_{s}|\Big]\Big)\mathrm{d}s+K\int_{0}^{t}e^{p\kappa s}\mathbb{E}\left[|X_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\right]\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq p\int_{0}^{t}(\kappa-\lambda+\varepsilon)\mathbb{E}\left[e^{\kappa ps}|X^{i,N}_{s}|^{p}\right]\mathrm{d}s+K\int_{0}^{t}e^{p\kappa s}\mathrm{d}s\leq Ke^{p\kappa t},

for some positive constant
K
and
\varepsilon>0
arbitrarily small.
∎

We provide the following auxiliary results:

Proposition A.1
.

Let the assumptions and set up of Proposition
2.1
hold and let the IPS be given as in (
2.4
). Then there exists
K\geq 0
, independent of
t,s
and
N
, such that for any
s\geq t\geq 0,~s-t<1

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{s}^{i,N}-X_{t}^{i,N}\big|^{2}\Big]\leq K(s-t).

Proof.

By Itô’s formula and an application of the Cauchy–Schwarz and Jensen inequalities, we have that for any
s\geq t\geq 0,~(s-t)<1
,
i\in\{1,\dots,N\}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[

\displaystyle\big|X_{s}^{i,N}-X_{t}^{i,N}\big|^{2}\Big]=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg|\int_{t}^{s}-\nabla U(X_{u}^{i,N})-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(X_{u}^{i,N}-X_{u}^{j,N})\mathrm{d}u+\sigma\int_{t}^{s}\mathrm{d}W_{u}^{i}\bigg|^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq K(s-t)\int_{t}^{s}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\nabla U(X_{u}^{i,N})\big|^{2}\Big]+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\nabla V(X_{u}^{i,N}-X_{u}^{j,N})\big|^{2}\Big]\mathrm{d}u+K(s-t)

\displaystyle\leq K(s-t)\Big(1+\int_{t}^{s}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{u}^{i,N}\big|^{2}\Big]+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{u}^{j,N}\big|^{2}\Big]\mathrm{d}u\Big)\leq K(s-t),

where we used Proposition
2.1
in the last estimate.
∎

A.2
Proof of Proposition
2.2

For clarity, we prove each of the proposition’s statements separately. The main argument requires care as an analysis by cases is needed, but within each case the estimation procedure is standard.

Proof of Proposition
2.2
– Statement (1): Moment estimates.

For the scheme defined in (
2.6
), we introduce the following notations for any
m\in\{0,\ldots,M\}
,

\displaystyle b_{m}^{i,N,h}\coloneqq-\nabla U(X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}})-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}),\quad\Delta\overline{W}_{m}^{i,N,h}\coloneqq\frac{\Delta W_{m}^{i}+\Delta W_{m+1}^{i}}{2}.

(A.2)

Assumption
1
implies that

\displaystyle\big(X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}\big)\cdot

\displaystyle\big(~b_{m}^{i,N,h}-b_{m}^{j,N,h}\big)\leq-\lambda\big|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{2},

\displaystyle\big|~b_{m}^{i,N,h}-b_{m}^{j,N,h}\big|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\big|\nabla U(X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}})-\nabla U(X^{j,N,h}_{t_{m}})\big|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\big|\nabla V(X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{k,N,h})-\nabla V(X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{k,N,h})\big|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2(\lambda^{2}+K_{V}^{2})|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\leq K|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}.

An inspection of the above inequalities indicates that in order to establish the
L^{p}
-moments for
X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}
, we need
L^{p}
-estimates on the local differences
X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}
(a term that appears from the interaction kernel). This proof is split accordingly;
Part 1
deals with the latter while
Part 2
with the former.

Part 1: Moments of local differences uniformly bounded in time.

We first prove that for all
i,j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
,
m\in\{0,\ldots,M\},p\geq 2
, with
p
an even number, that
\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]
is uniformly bounded in time. Note that due to the nature of the scheme,
X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}
is not independent of
\Delta\overline{W}_{m}^{i,N,h}
, and thus we analyze the different cases as time evolves, i.e.,
m\in\{0,1,2\}
and
m\geq 3
below. (This same procedure will be used in
Part 2
of the proof.)

Case:
m=0
.

For any
i,j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}|^{p}+|X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]=K\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big].

(A.3)

Case:
m=1
.

We get

\displaystyle|X_{t_{1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{1}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle=|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}+b_{0}^{i,N,h}h-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}-b_{0}^{j,N,h}h|^{2}+|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{j,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\big(X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}+b_{0}^{i,N,h}h-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}-b_{0}^{j,N,h}h\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{j,N,h}\big)

\displaystyle\leq|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\big(1-2\lambda h+Kh^{2}\big)+|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{j,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\big(X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}+b_{0}^{i,N,h}h-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}+b_{0}^{j,N,h}h\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{j,N,h}\big).

Taking the power
p/2
and expectation on both sides, we deduce that there exist positive constants
K_{p,1}
,
K_{p,2}
,
K
,
\kappa
(all are independent of
h,T,M
and
N
) such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{1}}^{i,N,h}-

\displaystyle X_{t_{1}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big||X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\big(1-2\lambda h+Kh^{2}\big)+|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{j,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\big(X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}+b_{0}^{i,N,h}h-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}+b_{0}^{j,N,h}h\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{j,N,h}\big)\Big|^{p/2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]\big(1-2\lambda h+Kh^{2}\big)^{p}

\displaystyle\quad+h\big(K_{p,1}\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]+K_{p,2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+p\mathbb{E}\big[|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\cdot|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}|^{p-2}\big(1-2\lambda h+Kh^{2}\big)^{p-2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq(1-K_{p,1}h)\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]+~K_{p,2}h\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p-2}\big]\big)

\displaystyle\leq K\big(h+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p-2}\big]\big)\leq Ke^{\kappa t_{1}}\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-{\kappa t_{1}}}\big),

where we keep the
\mathcal{O}(1)
and
\mathcal{O}(h)
terms by expanding the formula in the first inequality and bound the
o(h)
terms by
\mathcal{O}(h)
terms via Young’s inequality.

Note that in this case (and the case
m=2
) the factor
e^{-\kappa t_{1}}
(
e^{-\kappa t_{2}}
for
m=2
) is only added to make it consistent with the estimates obtained for a general
m
.

Case:
m=2
.

Based on the calculations above, there exist positive constants
K,{\kappa}
(both are independent of
h,T,M
and
N
) such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{2}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\leq Ke^{\kappa t_{2}}(1+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-{\kappa}t_{2}}).

(A.4)

Note that below we will show that the constant on the right-hand side will not blow up as
m
increases.

Case:
m\geq 3
.

More generally, for
m\geq 3
, we have

\displaystyle|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-2\lambda h+Kh^{2}\big)|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}+\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}|^{2}

(A.5)

\displaystyle\quad+2\big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}+b_{m-1}^{i,N,h}h-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}+b_{m-1}^{j,N,h}h\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big).

Since for
m\geq 3
,
X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}
is not independent of
\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}
, we further expand
X_{t_{m-1}}^{\cdot,N,h}
to get

\displaystyle\big(

\displaystyle X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}+b_{m-1}^{i,N,h}h-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}+b_{m-1}^{j,N,h}h\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\big(X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}+b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h-b_{m-2}^{j,N,h}h+\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+Kh\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big|.

Define the following local quantities: for all
i,j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}

\displaystyle G_{m,1}^{i,j}

\displaystyle=\big(1-2\lambda h+Kh^{2}\big)|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}|^{2},\quad G_{m,2}^{i,j}=\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle G_{m,3}^{i,j}

\displaystyle=2\big(X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}+\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big),

\displaystyle G_{m,4}^{i,j}

\displaystyle=2\big(b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h-b_{m-2}^{j,N,h}h\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big)

\displaystyle G_{m,5}^{i,j}

\displaystyle=2Kh\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big|.

Using these local quantities, we can express the estimate (
A.5
) as follows:

\displaystyle|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big(G_{m,1}^{i,j}+G_{m,2}^{i,j}+G_{m,3}^{i,j}+G_{m,4}^{i,j}+G_{m,5}^{i,j}\big).

Now, taking the power of
p/2
and expectations on both sides, in combination with Young’s inequality, we have for some positive constants
K_{p,1},K_{p,2},K
( both are independent of
h,T,M
and
N
) such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[

\displaystyle\big(G_{m,1}^{i,j}+G_{m,2}^{i,j}+G_{m,3}^{i,j}+G_{m,4}^{i,j}+G_{m,5}^{i,j}\big)^{p/2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=0}^{p/2}\binom{p/2}{l}\mathbb{E}\Big[(G_{m,1}^{i,j})^{p/2-l}(G_{m,2}^{i,j}+G_{m,3}^{i,j}+G_{m,4}^{i,j}+G_{m,5}^{i,j})^{l}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[|G_{m,1}^{i,j}|^{p/2}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad+p\mathbb{E}\Big[|G_{m,1}^{i,j}|^{p/2-1}(G_{m,2}^{i,j}+G_{m,3}^{i,j}+G_{m,4}^{i,j}+G_{m,5}^{i,j})+|G_{m,1}^{i,j}|^{p/2-2}|G_{m,3}^{i,j}|^{2}\Big]

(A.6)

\displaystyle\qquad+h^{3/2}\Big(K_{p,1}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+K_{p,2}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+K\Big),

(A.7)

where we used the fact that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[(G_{m,1}^{i,j})^{p/2-2}(G_{m,2}^{i,j}+G_{m,3}^{i,j}+G_{m,4}^{i,j}+G_{m,5}^{i,j})^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[|G_{m,1}^{i,j}|^{p/2-2}|G_{m,3}^{i,j}|^{2}\Big]+h^{3/2}\Big(K\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+K\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+K\Big),

and the terms for
l\in\{3,\dots,p/2\}
are also bounded by the term in (
A.7
).
The (
A.6
) terms are estimated next:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[(G_{m,1}^{i,j})^{p/2-1}G_{m,5}^{i,j}\big]

\displaystyle\leq Kh\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}+b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h-b_{m-2}^{j,N,h}h+\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-1}\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big|\right]

\displaystyle\leq Kh\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-1}\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big|\big]+Kh^{3/2}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-1}\big]+Kh^{3/2}

\displaystyle\leq Kh^{3/2}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-1}\big]+Kh^{3/2}.

Also, note that we have the bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[(G_{m,1}^{i,j})^{p/2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq(1-Kh)\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big].

In the following,
\varepsilon>0
(arbitrarily small) and
K>0
(both are independent of
h,T,M
and
N
) will denote positive constants appearing due to the application of Young’s inequality. In addition recall that
X_{t_{m-2}}^{\cdot,N,h}
is independent of
W_{m-1}^{\cdot,N,h}
. Consequently, we derive

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[

\displaystyle(G_{m,1}^{i,j})^{p/2-1}G_{m,2}^{i,j}\big]\leq(1-Kh)\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-2}\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq(1-Kh)\mathbb{E}\big[(K+\varepsilon\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p})\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq Kh+\varepsilon\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}+b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h-b_{m-2}^{j,N,h}h+\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq Kh+\varepsilon\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\big]+Kh^{3/2}

\displaystyle\quad+K\mathbb{E}\big[\big|b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h-b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h\big|^{p}\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\mathbb{E}\big[\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\big]\leq Kh+\varepsilon h\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big].

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[

\displaystyle(G_{m,1}^{i,j})^{p/2-1}(G_{m,3}^{i,j}+G_{m,4}^{i,j})\big]\leq Kh+\varepsilon h\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\quad+2(1-Kh)\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-2}\big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big)\big]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-1}\big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-1}\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h-b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h\big|^{p-1}\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon h\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+Kh+Kh^{3/2}

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-1}\big(X_{t_{m-3}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-3}}^{j,N,h}\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+K\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{t_{m-3}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-3}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-1}\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon h\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+Kh+Kh^{3/2}

\displaystyle\leq Kh+Kh^{3/2}+\varepsilon h\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-3}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-3}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+\varepsilon h\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big],

where we expanded the second term in the second inequality, used Young’s inequality and the fact that
X_{t_{m-3}}^{\cdot,N,h}
is independent of
W_{m-2}^{\cdot,N,h}
. For the last term, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\big[(G_{m,1}^{i,j})^{p/2-2}|G_{m,3}^{i,j}|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-4}\big(\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{2}+\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\cdot\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}+b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h-b_{m-2}^{j,N,h}h+\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p-4}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\cdot\big(\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{2}+\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{j,N,h}\big|^{2}\big)\cdot\big|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}-\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{j,N,h}\big|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\leq Kh+\varepsilon h\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big].

Hence, there exist positive constants
K_{p,3},K_{p,4},K_{p,5}
(all independent of
h,T,M
and
N
)
satisfying
K_{p,3}>2K_{p,4}

(by carefully choosing the constants in Young’s inequality) such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\big[|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\leq(1-K_{p,3}h)\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+K_{p,4}h\big(\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-2}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{m-3}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-3}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]\big)+K_{p,5}h

\displaystyle\leq K+Ke^{2\kappa}(1+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big])e^{-(K_{p,3}-2K_{p,4})t_{m}/3},

(A.8)

for some constant
K
(independent of
h,T,M
and
N
). In the last estimate, we used Lemma
A.7
with
c_{1}\equiv(1-K_{p,3}h),c_{2}\equiv K_{p,4}h,c_{3}\equiv K_{p,4}h
,
C\equiv K_{p,5}h
and the moment bounds for the differences process at
\{t_{0},t_{1},t_{2}\}
in (
A.3
) and (
A.4
). Note that the condition
0<c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3}<1
is satisfied for our choice
h\in(0,\min\{1/2\lambda,1\})
.

We conclude that there exist some positive constants
K,{\kappa}
(both independent of
h,T,M
and
N
), such that for all
i,j\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
, and
m\geq 0

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\big[|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}|^{p}\big]\leq K\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-{\kappa}t_{m}}\big).

(A.9)

Part 2: Moments are uniformly bounded in time.

Let
p\geq 2
be given. We now prove that for all
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\},m\geq 0,

\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]
is uniformly bounded in time. As in
Part 1
, we separately consider the cases
m\in\{0,1,2\}
then
m\geq 3
.

Let
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
be arbitrary and set
m=0
. Then we have by assumption on the initial data

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{0}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big]<\infty.

Case 1:
m=1
.

Due to Assumption
1
and Jensen’s inequality, it follows that

\displaystyle|X_{t_{1}}^{i,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle=|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}+b_{0}^{i,N,h}h|^{2}+|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}|^{2}+2\big(X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}+b_{0}^{i,N,h}h\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}\big)

\displaystyle\leq(1-2\lambda h+Kh^{2})|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}|^{2}+2\big(X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}+b_{0}^{i,N,h}h\big)\cdot\big(\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}\big)+|\sigma\Delta\overline{W}_{0}^{i,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2K_{V}h}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}||X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}|+\frac{K_{V}^{2}h^{2}}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}|X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{0}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}.

Raising to the power
p/2
and taking the expectation on both sides above, an application of (
A.9
), Jensen’s inequality and Young’s inequality shows that there exist positive constants
K_{p,6},\kappa
(both independent of
h,T,M
and
N
) such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{1}}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\leq e^{\kappa t_{1}}K_{p,6}\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-{\kappa}t_{1}}\big).

Case 2:
m=2
.

There exist positive constants
K_{p,6},\kappa
(both are independent of
h,T,M
and
N
) such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{2}}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\leq e^{\kappa t_{2}}K_{p,6}\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-{\kappa}t_{2}}\big).

We will show that the constant on the right-hand side does not blow up as
m
increases.

Case 3:
m\geq 3
.

We have

\displaystyle|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle=|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}+b_{m-1}^{i,N,h}h|^{2}+\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}|^{2}+2\big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}+b_{m-1}^{i,N,h}h\big)\cdot\big(\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}\big)

\displaystyle\leq(1-2\lambda h+Kh^{2})|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}|^{2}+\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}|^{2}+2\big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}+b_{m-1}^{i,N,h}h\big)\cdot\big(\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2K_{V}h}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}||X_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}|+\frac{K_{V}^{2}h^{2}}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}.

Since for
m\geq 3
,
X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}
is not independent of
\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}
, we further expand
X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}
and estimate

\displaystyle\big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}+b_{m-1}^{i,N,h}h\big)

\displaystyle\cdot\big(\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}\big)

\displaystyle\leq h|b_{m-1}^{i,N,h}||\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}|+\big(X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}+b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h+\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}\big)\cdot\big(\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}\big).

Note that
X_{t_{m-2}}^{\cdot,N,h}
is independent of
W_{m-1}^{\cdot,N,h}
. Similar to the analysis of the first part, we define the following local quantities: for all
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
,

\displaystyle G_{m,1}^{i}=(1-2\lambda h+Kh^{2})|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}|^{2},

\displaystyle G_{m,2}^{i}=\sigma^{2}|\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}|^{2},

\displaystyle G_{m,3}^{i}=2\big(X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}+b_{m-2}^{i,N,h}h+\Delta\overline{W}_{m-2}^{i,N,h}\big)\cdot\big(\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}\big),

\displaystyle G_{m,4}^{i}=2h|b_{m-1}^{i,N,h}||\Delta\overline{W}_{m-1}^{i,N,h}|,

\displaystyle G_{m,5}^{i}=\frac{2K_{V}h}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}||X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}|+\frac{K_{V}^{2}h^{2}}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}|^{2},

and note that

\displaystyle|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big(G_{m,1}^{i}+G_{m,2}^{i}+G_{m,3}^{i}+G_{m,4}^{i}+G_{m,5}^{i}\big).

Raising to the power
p/2
and taking the expectation on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[

\displaystyle\big(G_{m,1}^{i}+G_{m,2}^{i}+G_{m,3}^{i}+G_{m,4}^{i}+G_{m,5}^{i}\big)^{p/2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=0}^{p/2}\binom{p/2}{l}\mathbb{E}\Big[(G_{m,1}^{i})^{p/2-l}(G_{m,2}^{i}+G_{m,3}^{i}+G_{m,4}^{i}+G_{m,5}^{i})^{l}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[|G_{m,1}^{i}|^{p/2}\big]+p\mathbb{E}\Big[(G_{m,1}^{i})^{p/2-1}(G_{m,2}^{i}+G_{m,3}^{i}+G_{m,4}^{i}+G_{m,5}^{i})+|G_{m,1}^{i}|^{p/2-2}|G_{m,3}^{i}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+h^{3/2}\Big(K_{p,1}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+K_{p,2}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+K\Big).

Using (
A.8
), we obtain the following estimate:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[

\displaystyle(G_{m,1}^{i})^{p/2-1}G_{m,5}^{i}\big]

\displaystyle\leq K\mathbb{E}\bigg[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}\big|^{p-2}\Big(\frac{2K_{V}h}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}||X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}|+\frac{K_{V}^{2}h^{2}}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}|^{2}\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq h\Big(\varepsilon\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+\frac{K}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon h\mathbb{E}\big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}\big|^{p}\big]+Kh(1+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-(K_{p,3}-2K_{p,4})t_{m}/3}).

The analysis of the other terms works similarly as in the proof of the first part. Hence,
we conclude that
there exists positive constants
K_{p,7},K_{p,8},K_{p,9}
(both independent of
h,T,M
and
N
)
satisfying
K_{p,7}>2K_{p,8}
and
e^{-(K_{p,3}-2K_{p,4})h}\neq 1-(K_{p,7}-2K_{p,8})h>0
(for sufficiently small
h
independent of
T,M,N
), such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\leq(1-K_{p,7}h)\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]+K_{p,8}h\big(\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{m-2}}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{t_{m-3}}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt+K_{p,9}h\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-(K_{p,3}-2K_{p,4})t_{m}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq K+K(1+\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big])\big(e^{-(K_{p,7}-2K_{p,8})t_{m}/3}+e^{-(K_{p,3}-2K_{p,4})t_{m}/3}\big),

for some constant
K
(independent of
h,T,M
and
N
). In the last estimate, we used the second statement in Lemma
A.7
with
c_{1}\equiv(1-K_{p,7}h),c_{2}\equiv K_{p,8}h,c_{3}\equiv K_{p,8}h
,
c_{4}=K_{p,9}\mathbb{E}\big[|\xi|^{p}\big]h
,
c_{5}=K_{p,3}-2K_{p,4}
,
C\equiv K_{p,9}h
and the moment bounds at
\{t_{0},t_{1},t_{2}\}
.
∎

Proof of Proposition
2.2
– Statement (2): the
L^{2}
-strong error.

Consider the IPS described in (
2.4
) and recall the auxiliary scheme of (
6.39
).
For all
m\in\{0,\ldots,M-1\}
with
\hat{X}_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}=X^{i,N}_{t_{0}}
, we define

\displaystyle\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m+1}}=\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}-\Big(\nabla U(\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)h+\sigma\Delta W_{m}^{i},

where
t_{m}:=mh
,
T:=Mh
, and
\Delta W_{m}^{i}=W^{i}_{t_{m}}-W^{i}_{t_{m-1}}
. Recall that
X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}=\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}+\sigma\Delta W_{m}^{i}/2
.
We compute the difference terms:

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,m}:=X_{t_{m}}^{i,N}-\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m+1}}

(A.10)

\displaystyle=X_{t_{m}}^{i,N}-\bigg(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\Big(\nabla U(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)h+\sigma\Delta W_{m}^{i}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\Big(\nabla U(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)h+\sigma\Delta W_{m}^{i}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad-\bigg(\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}-\Big(\nabla U(\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)h+\sigma\Delta W_{m}^{i}\bigg)

\displaystyle=:R^{i,1}_{t_{m}}+R^{i,2}_{t_{m}}.

Note that here we match
X_{t_{m}}^{i,N}
with
\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m+1}}
instead of
\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}
.

We estimate the above terms separately and collect all the estimates an the end. For the first term, taking squares and expectations yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\big[|R^{i,1}_{t_{m}}|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big|~X_{t_{m}}^{i,N}-\bigg(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\Big(\nabla U(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)h+\sigma\Delta W_{m}^{i}\bigg)~\Big|^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big|-\int_{t_{m-1}}^{t_{m}}~\Big(\nabla U(X_{s}^{i,N})-\nabla U(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(X_{s}^{i,N}-X_{s}^{j,N})-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)\mathrm{d}s~\Big|^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq 2h\int_{t_{m-1}}^{t_{m}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\nabla U(X_{s}^{i,N})-\nabla U(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})\big|^{2}\Big]\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\quad+2h\int_{t_{m-1}}^{t_{m}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\nabla V(X_{s}^{i,N}-X_{s}^{j,N})-\nabla V(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\big|^{2}~\mathrm{d}s\Big]

\displaystyle\leq Kh\int_{t_{m-1}}^{t_{m}}\bigg(\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{s}^{i,N}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}\big|^{2}\Big]+\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}\big|^{2}\Big]+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\Big(\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{s}^{j,N}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}\big|^{2}\Big]+\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j}\big|^{2}\Big]\Big)~\bigg)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq Kh\int_{t_{m-1}}^{t_{m}}\Big(\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{s}^{i,N}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}\big|^{2}\Big]+h\Big)\mathrm{d}s~\leq Kh^{2}\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{2}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big),

where we used Jensen’s inequality, the Lipschitz continuity of the potentials and Proposition
2.1
,
\kappa,K>0
are independent of
h,T,M
and
N
.
Next, we consider the second term
R^{i,2}_{t_{m}}
. Taking squares and expectations, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\big[|R^{i,2}_{t_{m}}|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg|~\Big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\Big(\nabla U(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)h\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-\Big(\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}-\Big(\nabla U(\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)h\Big)~\bigg|^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}\big|^{2}\Big]-h\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}\Big)\cdot\Big(\nabla U(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})-\nabla U(\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{h}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}\Big)\cdot\Big(\nabla V(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad-\nabla V(\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+2h^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\nabla U(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})-\nabla U(\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})\big|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2h^{2}}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\nabla V(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad-\nabla V(\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\big|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}\big|^{2}\Big]-h\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}\Big)\cdot\Big(\nabla U(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})-\nabla U(\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i})\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+Kh^{2}\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{2}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big)-\frac{h}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\qquad\cdot\Big(\nabla V(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})-\nabla V(\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)\Big],

where we used Jensen’s inequality, Propositions
2.1
and the Statement (1) in Proposition
2.2
.
We further estimate

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|R^{i,2}_{t_{m}}|^{2}]\leq(1-\lambda h)\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}-\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}\big|^{2}\Big]+Kh^{2}\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{2}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{h}{2N^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big((X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})-(\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\cdot\Big(\nabla V(X_{t_{m-1}}^{i,N}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-X_{t_{m-1}}^{j,N}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})-\nabla V(\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}-\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h}-\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{j})\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\lambda h)\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\Delta_{i,m-1}\big|^{2}\Big]+Kh^{2}\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{2}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big),

with
\Delta_{i,m}
as defined in (
A.10
).
We used the
‘symmetrization trick’

in (
4.5
) to handle the convolution term.
Note that the positive constant
K
is independent of
h,T,M
and
N
.
Hence for all
m\in\{1,\ldots,M-1\},~i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
, there exists a positive constant
K
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\Delta_{i,m}\big|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\lambda h)\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\Delta_{i,m-1}\big|^{2}\Big]+Kh^{2}\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{2}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big)

\displaystyle=(1-\lambda h)^{m}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\Delta_{i,0}\big|^{2}\Big]+\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{2}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big)Kh^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}(1-\lambda h)^{j}

\displaystyle\leq\Big(Kh+\frac{Kh}{\lambda}\Big)\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{2}\big]\big),

where we used
\mathbb{E}\big[|\Delta_{i,0}|^{2}\big]\leq Kh\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{2}\big]
. Recall that
X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}=\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}+\sigma\Delta W_{m}^{i}/2
. Using Statement (1) in Proposition
2.2
and (
2.6
), we further have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}-\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}\big|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\frac{\sigma}{2}\Delta W_{m}^{i}\big|^{2}\Big]\leq Kh,

\displaystyle\textrm{and}\quad\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m+1}}-X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}\big|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq Kh\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{2}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big).

Collecting the last 3 estimates, we have for all
m\in\{1,\ldots,M-1\},~i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X_{t_{m}}^{i,N}-X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}\big|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\Delta_{i,m}+(\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m+1}}-X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m+1}})+(X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m+1}}-X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}})\big|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq K\Big(\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\Delta_{i,m}\big|^{2}\Big]+\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m+1}}-\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m+1}}\big|^{2}\Big]+\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m+1}}-X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}\big|^{2}\Big]\Big)

\displaystyle\leq Kh\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{2}\big]\big).

As
K
is independent of the critical quantities
M
and
N
, maximizing over
i
and
m
yields the final result.
∎

A.3
Auxiliary results

The following statement is an auxiliary result on the differences of SDE starting at different times (
t
and
s
with
t\leq s
) at the same point
\bm{x}
and is used in the proof of Proposition
4.3
.

Lemma A.2
.

Let the assumptions and setup of Proposition
4.3
hold and let
r\geq s\geq t\geq 0
,
u\geq 0
with
s-t<1
. Let the starting positions
x_{i}\in L^{4}(\Omega,\mathbb{R})
be
\mathcal{F}_{t}
-measurable random variables that are identically distributed over all
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
.
Let
(\bm{X}^{t,\bm{x},N}_{s})_{s\geq t\geq 0}
and
(\bm{X}^{s,\bm{x},N}_{r})_{r\geq s\geq 0}
be the solutions of (
3.2
) starting from
\bm{x}
at time
t
and
s
, respectively.
Then there exist some
\lambda_{2}\in(0,\min\{\lambda-2K_{V},\lambda_{1}\})
and
K>0
(both are independent of
s,t,N
), such that for any
i\in\{1,\dots,N\}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u}\big|^{4}\Big]\leq K(s-t)^{2}e^{-4\lambda_{2}u}.

Proof.

By Itô’s formula, we have, for any
s\geq t\geq 0
,
u\geq 0
,
i\in\{1,\dots,N\}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u}\big|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s}\big|^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad-4\int_{0}^{u}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+w}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+w}\big|^{2}\Big(X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+w}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+w}\Big)\cdot\bigg(~\Big(\nabla U(X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+w})-\nabla U(X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+w})\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}\nabla V(X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+w}-X^{t,x_{l},l,N}_{s+w})-\nabla V(X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+w}-X^{s,x_{l},l,N}_{s+w})\Big)\bigg)\Big]\mathrm{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s}\big|^{4}\Big]-4\Big(\lambda-\left(1+\frac{3}{4}\right)K_{V}\Big)\int_{0}^{u}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+w}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+w}\big|^{4}\Big]\mathrm{d}w

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{K_{V}}{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{u}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{t,x_{l},l,N}_{s+w}-X^{s,x_{l},l,N}_{s+w}\big|^{4}\Big]\mathrm{d}w,

where we used Young’s inequality along with Assumption
1
.
From the proof of Proposition
4.3
(see, equation
4.24
), we deduce that
\mathbb{E}\big[|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s}|^{4}\big]\leq K(s-t)^{2}
. Using the fact that
\lambda>2K_{V}
, we conclude the claim.
∎

The next statement concerning the second-order variation process is similar to Proposition
4.3
which is used in the proof in Lemma
6.1
.

Proposition A.3
.

Let the assumptions and set up of Lemma
4.2
and Proposition
4.3
hold.
Then there exist some
\lambda_{4}\in(0,\min\{\lambda-2K_{V},\lambda_{3}\})
and

K>0
(both are independent of
h,T,M
and
N
)
such that
for all
T\geq s\geq t\geq 0
(with
s-t<1
) and
i\in\{1,\dots,N\}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{i},x_{i}}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{i},x_{i}}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq K(s-t)e^{-{2\lambda_{4}}(T-s)},

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j,k=1,i\neq j\neq k}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Big[|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{j},x_{k}}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{j},x_{k}}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K(s-t)}{N}e^{-2\lambda_{4}(T-s)},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i,k=1,i\neq k}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Big[|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{k},x_{k}}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{k},x_{k}}|^{2}+|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{i},x_{k}}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{i},x_{k}}|^{2}+|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{k},x_{i}}

\displaystyle-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{k},x_{i}}|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq K(s-t)e^{-2\lambda_{4}(T-s)}.

Proof.

This proof is a combination of the methods used to prove Proposition
4.3
and Lemma
4.4
and we streamline the presentation. For any
i,j,k\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
, we have that

\displaystyle|

\displaystyle X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{j},x_{k}}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{T,x_{j},x_{k}}|^{2}=|X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s,x_{j},x_{k}}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s,x_{j},x_{k}}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad-2\int_{0}^{T-s}\Big(X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u,x_{j}}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u,x_{j}}\Big)\cdot\Big(\nabla^{2}U(X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u})X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u,x_{j},x_{k}}-\nabla^{2}U(X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u})X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u,x_{j},x_{k}}\Big)\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\quad\quad-2\int_{0}^{T}\Big(X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u,x_{j},x_{k}}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u,x_{j},x_{k}}\Big)\cdot\Big(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}\nabla^{2}V(X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u}-X^{t,x_{l},l,N}_{s+u})(X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{{s+u},x_{j},x_{k}}-X^{t,x_{l},l,N}_{{s+u},x_{j},x_{k}})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\qquad\qquad-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}\nabla^{2}V(X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u}-X^{s,x_{l},l,N}_{s+u})(X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{{s+u},x_{j},x_{k}}-X^{s,x_{l},l,N}_{{s+u},x_{j},x_{k}})\Big)\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\quad\quad-2\int_{0}^{T-s}\Big(X^{t,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u,x_{j},x_{k}}-X^{s,x_{i},i,N}_{s+u,x_{j},x_{k}}\Big)\cdot\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{N}\sum_{l^{\prime}=1}^{N}\partial^{2}_{x_{l},x_{l^{\prime}}}B_{i}(\bm{X}_{u}^{t,\bm{x},N})X^{t,x_{l},l,N}_{s+u,x_{j}}X^{t,x_{l^{\prime}},l^{\prime},N}_{s+u,x_{k}}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad\qquad\quad\quad-\sum_{l=1}^{N}\sum_{l^{\prime}=1}^{N}\partial^{2}_{x_{l},x_{l^{\prime}}}B_{i}(\bm{X}_{u}^{s,\bm{x},N})X^{s,x_{l},l,N}_{s+u,x_{j}}X^{s,x_{l^{\prime}},l^{\prime},N}_{s+u,x_{k}}\Big)~\mathrm{d}u.

The remaining steps are similar to those in the proof of Lemma
4.4
and Proposition
4.3
and we therefore omit a detailed analysis.
∎

The next statement is a classical result on the stability of SDEs with respect their initial condition.

Lemma A.4
.

Let Assumption
1
hold (with
\lambda>0
denoting the convexity parameter) and let
p\geq 2
be given. Let
(\bm{X}^{N}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
, and
(\bm{Y}^{N}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
be generated from (
2.4
) with i.i.d. initial states
X^{i,N}_{0}\sim\mu,Y^{i,N}_{0}\sim\nu\in\mathcal{P}_{p}(\mathbb{R})
, where
\bm{X}^{N}_{0}=(X^{1,N}_{0},\ldots,X^{N,N}_{0})
and
\bm{Y}^{N}_{0}=(Y^{1,N}_{0},\ldots,Y^{N,N}_{0})
. Then for any
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
,
t\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N}_{t}-Y^{i,N}_{t}\big|^{2}\Big]\leq e^{-2\lambda t}\mathbb{E}\Big[\,\big|X^{i,N}_{0}-Y^{i,N}_{0}\big|^{2}\Big].

(A.11)

Proof.

This result is classical and we present only a sketch of its proof. By Itô’s formula, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N}_{t}-Y^{i,N}_{t}\big|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N}_{0}-Y^{i,N}_{0}\big|^{2}\Big]-2\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(X^{i,N}_{s}-Y^{i,N}_{s}\Big)\cdot\Big(\nabla U(X^{i,N}_{s})-\nabla U(Y^{i,N}_{s})\Big)\Big]\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\qquad-2\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(X^{i,N}_{s}-Y^{i,N}_{s}\Big)\cdot\Big(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}\nabla V(X^{i,N}_{s}-X^{l,N}_{s})-\nabla V(Y^{i,N}_{s}-Y^{l,N}_{s})\Big)\Big]\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N}_{0}-Y^{i,N}_{0}\big|^{2}\Big]-2\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N}_{s}-Y^{i,N}_{s}\big|^{2}\Big]\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{2N^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{l=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big((X^{i,N}_{s}-X^{l,N}_{s})-(Y^{i,N}_{s}-Y^{l,N}_{s})\Big)\cdot\Big(\nabla V(X^{i,N}_{s}-X^{l,N}_{s})-\nabla V(Y^{i,N}_{s}-Y^{l,N}_{s})\Big)\Big]\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N}_{0}-Y^{i,N}_{0}\big|^{2}\Big]-2\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|X^{i,N}_{s}-Y^{i,N}_{s}\big|^{2}\Big]\mathrm{d}s,

where we used Assumption
1
. This estimate allows to deduce the first result (
A.11
).

∎

Lemma A.5
.

Let the assumptions and set up of Proposition
2.2

hold with
\xi\in L^{p}(\Omega,\mathbb{R})
for some given
p\geq 2
.
Then for the processes defined in (
6.37
),
(
6.39
) and (
6.40
), respectively, there exists a constant

K>0
(independent of
h,T,M
and
N
)
such that
for all
m\in\{0,\ldots,M-1\}

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\sup_{s\in[0,h]}\mathbb{E}\big[\,|X_{t_{m}+s}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]\leq K\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big),

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}|^{p}\big]\leq K\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big),

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\sup_{s\in[0,h)}\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\overline{X}_{t_{m}+s}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]\leq K\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big).}

Proof.

Using Proposition
2.2
and taking the definition of the processes in (
6.37
) into account, we have for all
m\in\{0,\ldots,M-1\},~s\in[0,h]
and
i\in\{1,\dots,N\}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\,|X_{t_{m}+s}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]\leq

\displaystyle K\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[\,|X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}|^{p}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\nabla U(X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}})|^{p}\big]h^{p}+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\nabla V(X_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}-X_{t_{m}}^{j,N,h})|^{p}\big]h^{p}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\Delta W_{m}^{i}|^{p}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\Delta W_{m+1,s}^{i}|^{p}\big]\Big)

\displaystyle\leq K(1+h^{p})\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[\,|X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}|^{p}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\nabla U(X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}})|^{p}\big]+\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\nabla U(X^{j,N,h}_{t_{m}})|^{p}\big]+1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq K\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big),

where we used Jensen’s inequality and the fact that
\nabla U,\nabla V
are of linear growth (Assumption
1
).

For the second and the last estimate, recall that
X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}=\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}+\sigma\Delta W_{m}^{i}/2
and
\overline{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m-1}+s}=\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}+\sigma\Delta W_{m,s}^{i}/2
. We have that for all
m\in\{1,\ldots,M-1\},~s\in[0,h)
(recall
h\in(0,1)
is sufficiently small) and
i\in\{1,\dots,N\}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\hat{X}_{t_{m}}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\leq K\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\,|X^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}|^{p}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\Delta W_{m}^{i}|^{p}\big]\big)\leq K\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m}}\big),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\hat{X}_{t_{0}}^{i,N,h}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big[\,|X^{i,N}_{t_{0}}|^{p}\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{p}\big],

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\overline{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m-1}+s}|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\leq K\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\hat{X}^{i,N,h}_{t_{m}}|^{p}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\Delta W_{m,s}^{i}|^{p}\big]\big)

\displaystyle\leq K\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m-1}}e^{-\kappa h}\big)

\displaystyle\leq K\big(1+\mathbb{E}\big[\,|\xi|^{p}\big]e^{-\kappa t_{m-1}}\big).

∎

Lemma A.6
.

(Gronwall’s inequality). Let
T>0
and let
\alpha,\beta
and
u
be real-valued functions defined on
[0,T]
. Assume that
\alpha
and
u
are continuous and that the negative part of
\beta
is integrable on every closed and bounded subinterval of
[0,T]
.
If
\alpha
is non-negative and if
u
satisfies the integral inequality

\displaystyle u(t)\leq\beta(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\alpha(s)u(s)\mathrm{d}s,\quad\forall t\in[0,T],

then

\displaystyle u(t)\leq\beta(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\alpha(s)\beta(s)\exp\left(\int_{s}^{t}\alpha(r)\mathrm{d}r\right)\mathrm{d}s,\quad\forall t\in[0,T].

If we further have that
\beta
is non-decreasing, then

\displaystyle u(t)\leq\beta(t)\exp\left(\int_{0}^{t}\alpha(s)\mathrm{d}s\right),\quad\forall t\in[0,T].

The following auxiliary result is needed in the proof of Proposition
2.2
.

Lemma A.7
.

Let
c_{1},c_{2},c_{3},c_{4},c_{5}>0,C>0
be real constants with
c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3}<1
. Let
(a_{n})_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
be a real-valued sequence satisfying
a_{n+3}\leq c_{3}a_{n+2}+c_{2}a_{n+1}+c_{1}a_{n}+C
with initial values
0<a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}<K
, for some constant
K>0
. Then there exist some constants
K_{1},K_{2}>0
(both are independent of
n
) such that for all
n\geq 4

\displaystyle a_{n}\leq K_{1}+K_{2}\max\{a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\}e^{-(1-c_{1}-c_{2}-c_{3})n/3},

Moreover, if
a_{n+3}\leq c_{3}a_{n+2}+c_{2}a_{n+1}+c_{1}a_{n}+C+c_{4}e^{-c_{5}n}
and
(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\neq e^{-c_{5}}
, then there exists constants
K_{3},K_{4}>0
(both are independent of
n
) such that for all
n\geq 4

\displaystyle a_{n}\leq K_{3}+K_{4}\max\{a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\}e^{-(1-c_{1}-c_{2}-c_{3})n/3}.

Proof.

By the condition satisfied by the sequence
(a_{n})_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
, we deduce for any
n\geq 1

\displaystyle a_{n+3}

\displaystyle\leq c_{3}a_{n+2}+c_{2}a_{n+1}+c_{1}a_{n}+C\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\max\{a_{n},a_{n+1},a_{n+2}\}+C,

\displaystyle a_{n+4}

\displaystyle\leq c_{3}a_{n+3}+c_{2}a_{n+2}+c_{1}a_{n+1}+C\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\max\{a_{n+1},a_{n+2},a_{n+3}\}+C

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\max\big\{a_{n+1},a_{n+2},(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\max\{a_{n},a_{n+1},a_{n+2}\}+C\big\}+C

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\max\{a_{n},a_{n+1},a_{n+2}\}+2C,

\displaystyle a_{n+5}

\displaystyle\leq c_{3}a_{n+4}+c_{2}a_{n+3}+c_{1}a_{n+2}+C\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\max\{a_{n+2},a_{n+3},a_{n+4}\}+C

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\max\{a_{n},a_{n+1},a_{n+2}\}+3C.

(A.12)

Hence,

\displaystyle\max\{a_{n+3},a_{n+4},a_{n+5}\}

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\max\{a_{n},a_{n+1},a_{n+2}\}+3C.

Consequently, adding
\tfrac{3C}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-1}<0
on both sides, we observe

\displaystyle\max\{a_{n+3},

\displaystyle a_{n+4},a_{n+5}\}+\frac{3C}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-1}

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\Big(\max\{a_{n},a_{n+1},a_{n+2}\}+\frac{3C}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-1}\Big).

Further, we derive for
n\geq 1

\displaystyle\max\{a_{3n+1},

\displaystyle a_{3n+2},a_{3n+3}\}+\frac{3C}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-1}

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})^{n}\Big(\max\{a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\}+\frac{3C}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-1}\Big),

which implies

\displaystyle\max\{a_{3n+1},a_{3n+2},a_{3n+3}\}

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})^{n}\max\{a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\}+\frac{3C}{1-(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-(1-c_{1}-c_{2}-c_{3})n}\max\{a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\}+K,

for some
K>0
,
where we used the inequality
e^{x}\geq 1+x
, for any
x\in\mathbb{R}
and
c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3}<1
.

Similarly, for the second claim, using the fact that
e^{-c_{5}(n+2)}<e^{-c_{5}(n+1)}<e^{-c_{5}n}
, we derive as in (
A.12
):

\displaystyle\max\{a_{n+3},a_{n+4},a_{n+5}\}

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\max\{a_{n},a_{n+1},a_{n+2}\}+3C+3c_{4}e^{-c_{5}n}.

Adding
\frac{3C}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-1}+\frac{3e^{-c_{5}(n+1)}}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-e^{-c_{5}}}
on both sides, we observe that

\displaystyle\max\{a_{3n+1},a_{3n+2},a_{3n+3}\}+\frac{3C}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-1}+\frac{3e^{-c_{5}(n+1)}}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-e^{-c_{5}}}

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})\Big(\max\{a_{3n-2},a_{3n-1},a_{3n}\}+\frac{3C}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-1}+\frac{3e^{-c_{5}n}}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-e^{-c_{5}}}\Big).

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\max\{a_{3n+1},a_{3n+2},a_{3n+3}\}+\frac{3C}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-1}+\frac{3e^{-c_{5}(n+1)}}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-e^{-c_{5}}}

\displaystyle\leq(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})^{n}\Big(\max\{a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\}+\frac{3C}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-1}+\frac{3e^{-c_{5}}}{(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-e^{-c_{5}}}\Big),

and therefore

\displaystyle\max\{a_{3n+1},a_{3n+2},a_{3n+3}\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq e^{-(1-c_{1}-c_{2}-c_{3})n}\max\{a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\}+\frac{3C}{1-(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})}+\frac{3e^{-c_{5}}}{|(c_{1}+c_{2}+c_{3})-e^{-c_{5}}|}(1-e^{-c_{5}n})

\displaystyle\quad\leq e^{-(1-c_{1}-c_{2}-c_{3})n}\max\{a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}\}+\tilde{K}_{2},

for some positive constant
\tilde{K}_{2}
.
∎

A.4
Auxiliary results from Section
6.1

Lemma A.8
.

Recall the operator
L
given in (
6.2
) and
\mu^{N,*}(\bm{x})
given by (
6.6
), then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[L(0,\overline{\bm{X}}_{0}^{N})\Big]=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}L(0,\bm{x})\mu^{N,*}(\mathrm{d}\bm{x})=0.

(A.13)

The proof relies on using integration by parts. The argument is found in
[
43
, Equation (3.29)]
and leverages the known closed form (up to a scaling constant) of the invariant density map
\mu^{N,*}
and the definition of
L
.

Proof.

Recall the expression for
B_{i}(\bm{x})
in (
2.3
)

\displaystyle B_{i}(\bm{x})=B_{i}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{N})\coloneqq-\nabla U(x_{i})-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\nabla V(x_{i}-x_{j}).

Define
H:\mathbb{R}^{N}\to N
for
\bm{x}=(x_{1},\ldots,x_{N})\in\mathbb{R}^{N}
as

\displaystyle H(\bm{x}):=\sum_{k=1}^{N}U(x_{k})+\frac{1}{2N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{\ell=1}^{N}V(x_{k}-x_{\ell}),

then we can write
\mu^{N,*}(\bm{x})
in (
6.6
) as

\mu^{N,*}(\bm{x})\propto\exp\big(-2H(\bm{x})/\sigma^{2}\big)
.
Since
V
is assumed even, we have that
\nabla V
is odd and thus
\partial_{x_{i}}H(\bm{x})=-B_{i}(\bm{x})
.

Let us now focus on the operator
L
given in (
6.2
). Note that it can be written as
L=\frac{1}{2}(L_{1}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}L_{2})
where
L_{1}
captures the first summand in
L
and
L_{2}
captures the remaining two terms of
L
that are multiplied by the weights
\sigma^{2}/2
.

To prove (
A.13
), we use integration by parts and show that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle L_{2}(0,\bm{x})\mu^{N,*}(\mathrm{d}\bm{x})=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}L_{1}(0,\bm{x})\mu^{N,*}(\mathrm{d}\bm{x})\Leftrightarrow

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle\bigg(\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\partial_{x_{j}}B_{i}(\bm{x})\partial_{x_{i},x_{j}}^{2}u(t,\bm{x})+\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\partial^{2}_{x_{j},x_{j}}B_{i}(\bm{x})\partial_{x_{i}}u(t,\bm{x})\bigg)\exp\Big(-\frac{2}{\sigma^{2}}H(\bm{x})\Big)\mathrm{d}\bm{x}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}\bigg(\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}B_{j}(\bm{x})\partial_{x_{j}}B_{i}(\bm{x})\partial_{x_{i}}u(t,\bm{x})\bigg)\exp\Big(-\frac{2}{\sigma^{2}}H(\bm{x})\Big)\mathrm{d}\bm{x}.

Recognizing the sum terms (in the mid-line) as the product rule for derivatives, we have using integration by parts and the identity
\partial_{x_{j}}H(\bm{x})=-B_{j}(\bm{x})

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}L_{2}(0,\bm{x})\mu^{N,*}(\mathrm{d}\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bigg(\partial_{x_{j}}\Big(\partial_{x_{j}}B_{i}(\bm{x})\partial_{x_{i}}u(0,\bm{x})\Big)\bigg)\exp\Big(-\frac{2}{\sigma^{2}}H(\bm{x})\Big)\mathrm{d}\bm{x}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N-1}}\Bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\bigg(\partial_{x_{j}}B_{i}(\bm{x})\partial_{x_{i}}u(t,\bm{x})\bigg)\exp\Big(-\frac{2}{\sigma^{2}}H(\bm{x})\Big)\Bigg)\bigg|_{x_{j}=-\infty}^{x_{j}=+\infty}\mathrm{d}\bm{x}^{-j}

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}\Big(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{2}\partial_{x_{j}}B_{i}(\bm{x})\partial_{x_{i}}u(t,\bm{x})\Big)\partial_{x_{j}}\bigg(\exp\Big(-\frac{2}{\sigma^{2}}H(\bm{x})\Big)\bigg)\mathrm{d}\bm{x}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}\Big(B_{j}(\bm{x})\partial_{x_{j}}B_{i}(\bm{x})\partial_{x_{i}}u(t,\bm{x})\Big)\exp\Big(-\frac{2}{\sigma^{2}}H(\bm{x})\Big)\mathrm{d}\bm{x}=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}L_{1}(0,\bm{x})\mu^{N,*}(\mathrm{d}\bm{x}),

where the integration over
\mathrm{d}\bm{x}^{-j}
means that we have integrated the variable
x_{j}
out and
N-1
integrations remain; the boundary term (over the
x_{j}
variable) is zero due to the boundedness assumptions of the derivatives of
u
and
B
and that (
6.6
) is a density that vanishes at infinity.
∎

A.5
Omitted residual terms of Section
6.2

In this part, we show the exact expectation form for the residual terms
R^{2}_{t_{m}},R^{3}_{t_{m}},R^{5}_{t_{m}},R^{7}_{t_{m}}
and
R^{8}_{t_{m}}
in Lemma
6.2
. The positive constant
K
below is independent of
h,T,M
and
N
and may have a different value in each line.
For the residual term
R^{2}_{t_{m}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[R^{2}_{t_{m}}\big]=K\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{2}}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\cdot\partial^{5}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u\Big(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{3}}^{N,h})\Big)\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{3}}\ \mathrm{d}{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{3}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+K\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{2}}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\cdot\partial^{6}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u\Big(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{3}}^{N,h})\Big)\mathrm{d}{q_{3}}\ \mathrm{d}{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{3}}_{q_{1}}\bigg].

For the residual term
R^{3}_{t_{m}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\big[R^{3}_{t_{m}}\big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Kh\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\qquad\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\cdot\partial_{x_{\gamma_{1}}}\bigg(\partial_{x_{\gamma_{3}}}B_{\gamma_{2}}(\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\partial^{2}_{x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\bigg)\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{3}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+Kh\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\qquad\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\cdot\partial^{2}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}}}\bigg(\partial_{x_{\gamma_{3}}}B_{\gamma_{2}}(\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\partial^{2}_{x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\bigg)\mathrm{d}{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{3}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+Kh\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\qquad\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\cdot\partial_{x_{\gamma_{1}}}\bigg(B_{\gamma_{2}}(\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\partial^{3}_{x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\bigg)\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{3}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+Kh\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\qquad\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\cdot\partial^{2}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}}}\bigg(B_{\gamma_{2}}(\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\partial^{3}_{x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\bigg)\mathrm{d}{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{3}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+K\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{2}}\qquad\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{5}^{N}}\bigg(\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{4}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{5}}\cdot\partial^{5}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},\dots,x_{\gamma_{5}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{3}}^{N,h})~\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{3}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{2}}_{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{3}}_{q_{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{4}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{5}}\cdot\partial^{6}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}},x_{\gamma_{5}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{3}}^{N,h})~\mathrm{d}{q_{3}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{2}}_{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{3}}_{q_{1}}\bigg)~\bigg]

\displaystyle+K\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\qquad\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{4}^{N}}\bigg(\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{4}}\cdot\partial^{5}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\mathrm{d}{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{2}}_{q_{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{4}}\cdot\partial^{5}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}{q_{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{4}}\cdot\partial^{6}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\mathrm{d}{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}{q_{1}}\bigg)~\bigg].

For the residual term
R^{5}_{t_{m}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[

\displaystyle R^{5}_{t_{m}}\big]=\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{5}^{N}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\partial_{x_{\gamma_{1}}}\bigg(\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{2}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{4}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{5}}\cdot{\partial^{4}_{x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}},x_{\gamma_{5}}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})\bigg)\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{4}^{N}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\partial^{2}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}}}\bigg(\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{2}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{4}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{5}}\cdot\partial^{4}_{x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}},x_{\gamma_{5}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})\bigg)\mathrm{d}{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+Kh\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{4}^{N}}\mathbb{E}\Big[B_{\gamma_{1}}({\bm{X}}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{2}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{4}}\cdot\partial^{4}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}}}u(t_{m+1},\bm{X}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+Kh^{2}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{4}^{N}}\mathbb{E}\Big[B_{\gamma_{1}}({\bm{X}}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})B_{\gamma_{2}}({\bm{X}}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{3}}\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{4}}\cdot\partial^{4}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}}}u(t_{m+1},\bm{X}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+Kh^{3}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{4}^{N}}\mathbb{E}\Big[B_{\gamma_{1}}({\bm{X}}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})B_{\gamma_{2}}({\bm{X}}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})B_{\gamma_{3}}({\bm{X}}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})\Delta W_{m,2h}^{\gamma_{4}}\cdot\partial^{4}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}}}u(t_{m+1},\bm{X}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+Kh^{4}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{4}^{N}}\mathbb{E}\Big[B_{\gamma_{1}}({\bm{X}}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})B_{\gamma_{2}}({\bm{X}}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})B_{\gamma_{3}}({\bm{X}}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})B_{\gamma_{4}}({\bm{X}}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})\cdot\partial^{4}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}}}u(t_{m+1},\bm{X}_{t_{m}}^{N,h})\Big].

For the residual term
R^{7}_{t_{m}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[R^{7}_{t_{m}}\big]=

\displaystyle Kh\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{2}}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{5}^{N}}\partial^{5}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}},x_{\gamma_{5}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{3}}^{N,h})\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{3}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{2}}_{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{3}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+Kh\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{2}}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{5}^{N}}\partial^{6}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}},,x_{\gamma_{5}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{3}}^{N,h})\mathrm{d}{q_{3}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{2}}_{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{3}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+Kh\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{4}^{N}}\partial^{5}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\mathrm{d}{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{2}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+Kh\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{4}^{N}}\partial^{5}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+Kh\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\int_{t_{m}}^{q_{1}}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{4}^{N}}\partial^{6}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{4}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{2}}^{N,h})\mathrm{d}{q_{2}}\ \mathrm{d}{q_{1}}\bigg].

For the residual term
R^{8}_{t_{m}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[R^{8}_{t_{m}}\big]

\displaystyle=Kh^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\partial_{x_{\gamma_{1}}}\bigg(B_{\gamma_{2}}(\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})\partial^{3}_{x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})\bigg)~\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+Kh^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\partial^{2}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}}}\bigg(B_{\gamma_{2}}(\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})\partial^{3}_{x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})\bigg)~\mathrm{d}{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+Kh^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\partial_{x_{\gamma_{1}}}\bigg(\partial_{x_{\gamma_{3}}}B_{\gamma_{2}}(\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})\partial^{2}_{x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})\bigg)~\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+Kh^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\partial^{2}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}}}\bigg(\partial_{x_{\gamma_{3}}}B_{\gamma_{2}}(\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})\partial^{2}_{x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})\bigg)~\mathrm{d}{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+Kh^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\partial^{5}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})~\mathrm{d}W^{\gamma_{1}}_{q_{1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+Kh^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int_{t_{m}}^{t_{m}+h}\sum_{\gamma\in\Pi_{3}^{N}}\partial^{6}_{x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{1}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{2}},x_{\gamma_{3}},x_{\gamma_{3}}}u(t_{m+1},\overline{\bm{X}}_{q_{1}}^{N,h})~\mathrm{d}{q_{1}}\bigg].
